Its799/800 implication\nstill requires the full-function W<=17/(16D_m), which is unproved. A triangle\nupper bound would not alone establish that full-function hypothesis.\n\n## Mechanism1: complete two-gap transport and exact region image\n\nLet q_i=(2/3)<x,v_i>, sort descending, and define the adjacent gaps u,v>=0.\nThen q=((2u+v)/3,(v-u)/3,-(u+2v)/3), A=(2u+v)/3,B=(u+2v)/3,\nand the exact squared norm is Q=u^2+uv+v^2.\n\nProjection guards: core u+2v<=1; vertex u>=1; edge u<=1,u+2v>=1.\nFor a physical h>1 put k=h-1. Psi=2F is Q in the core,2u+v-1 at a vertex,\nand(3u^2+2u+4v-1)/4 on an edge.\n\nFor G=max_i|<x,v_i>|=max(2u+v,u+2v)/2, the hexagon C_R={G<=R} retains both\nfacets. Exact nonlinear image calculation gives, for h>=2,R>=1,\n\n  sup_(x in C_R)G(T_h x)=max{h-1,R-h/2,(R+h-3/2)/2}.\n\nThe last term cannot be discarded. At the actual level2 bridge h=1+k,\nk=beta*c>2, take(u,v)=(1,3k/2). Its image is(k,k/2). This counterexample\nrefutes the concrete region erosion, not any terminal estimate.\n\n## Mechanism2: direction-dependent full suffix forecast\n\nFor a physical face chart f write P_D(x)=P_f x+b_f, with orthogonal projector\nP_f and perpendicular b_f. Core:(I,0); edge:(t t^T,n/2); vertex:(0,v_j).\nAt original time i set s_i=sum of all remaining h, p_i=product of all\nremaining(h-1), d_i=1+2s_i. Define at ANY point\n\n  L_i^f(x)=p_i^2||P_f x||^2+2<b_f,x>-d_i||b_f||^2.\n\nFor the ACTUAL present face f, L_i^f(x_i)=L_(i+1)^f(x_(i+1)). This does not\nassume any future guard. This separates the two targets.\n\n## Frozen word and true radius\n\nFix the SAME enclosing m>=1536 throughout:\n epsilon=1/(4m), beta=1-epsilon, c=(1+sqrt2)(1+epsilon), a=beta/c,\n H0=(), H_(j+1)=(H_j,1+a*c^j,H_j), S_j=sum H_j,\n n=2^m-1, D_m=1+2S_m.\nThe literal H2 is(A,B,A), A=1+a, B=1+beta. Every physical leaf, bridge,\nquery, and the actual terminal is retained.\n\nFor a compact convex D containing0,\n F_D(x)=max_(g in D)(<x,g>-||g||^2/2), grad F_D=P_D,\n U_d(x)=max_(g in D)(2<x,g>-d||g||^2).\nThe complete minimum set is K_D={z:<z,g><=0 for all g in D}.\nFor EVERY z_star in K_D, U_d(x)<=||x-z_star||^2/d; hence an eventual\nU block law would pay the true closest initial radius, not merely the\norigin norm. All original interpolation rows equal the same-body normal\n<xj-gj,gi-gj><=0. Scaling by L preserves W=2R.\n\nAccepted931b9653ce0f7201 remains conditional on a missing full-W bound.\nAccepted1252b8823bb5a290 supplies the original interpolation orientation.\nThe d=1 optimized two-cap witness is already in acceptedb98bb807d514e80d;\nthis report does not claim that construction as new.\n\n## Exact retained-normal identity and literal H2 debt\n\nFor xplus=x-hg, g=P_D(x), trailing d>=1, set\n Dmass=d+2h, p=P_D(x/Dmass), q=P_D(xplus/d),\n theta=h/(Dmass-1), z=theta*g+(1-theta)*q.\nThen EXACTLY\n\n U_(d+2h)(x)-U_d(xplus)\n =h*((2-h)*Dmass-2)/(Dmass-1)^2 *||q-g||^2\n  +2theta<x-g,g-q>\n  +Dmass||p-z||^2+2<x-Dmass*p,p-z>.\n\nAll three reserves on the last two lines are nonnegative. Both normals\nand the auxiliary mixture square are retained. All g,p,q,z lie in ONE D.\nFor a whole word use d_i=d+2 times its actual remaining mass and telescope.\nThis exact signed sum alone is not a positivity proof.\n\nFor actual H2=(A,B,A),\n d0=d+4A+2B, d1=d+2A+2B, d2=d+2A, d3=d.\nKeep physical g0,g1,g2 and auxiliary p0,p1,p2,p3. Both leaf coefficients\nare positive. The middle coefficient is B*(epsilon*d1-2)/(d1-1)^2.\nThus the entire H2 is paid for ALL bodies and starts when\n d>=max(1,2/epsilon-2(A+B)).\nBelow that threshold, the exact debt is\n B*(2-epsilon*d1)/(d1-1)^2 *||p2-g1||^2.\nIts payment by the actual correlated leaf, physical-normal, auxiliary-square\nand auxiliary-normal reserves remains unproved for arbitrary starts.\n\n## Sufficient shifted norm, exact two-child gluing, and its actual failure\n\nFor a whole word of mass S, input x and output y, put q=P_D(y/d).\nThe feasible single-body mixture\n [sum_i hi*gi+(d+S)q]/(d+2S)\ngives the quantitative bound\n\n U_(d+2S)(x)-U_d(y)\n >=[||x||^2-||y+S q||^2]/(d+2S).\n\nBoth ||P_D(y/d)||^2 and <y,P_D(y/d)> decrease with d. Therefore\n R_H(x)=||x||^2-||y+S P_D(y)||^2>=0\nwould suffice for ALL d>=1. It is stronger than U, not equivalent.\nThe report derives the exact single-step reserve and the following real-child\nidentity, rather than replacing actual child endpoints independently.\n\nLet x --H_j--> y --b--> z=y-bp --H_j--> t,\n p=P_D(y),q=P_D(t),w=p-q,e=z-(t+S q), S=S_j,b=1+a*c^j.\nDefine Rleft=||x||^2-||y+Sp||^2 and Rright=||z||^2-||t+Sq||^2.\nLet Ny=<y-p,p-q>, Nt=<t-q,q-p>, NR=-<t-q,e>.\nThese are actual nonnegative normals; NR is the terminal normal summed\nagainst every physical right-child gradient. Then\n\n Rparent=Rleft+(2S+b+1)/(S+1)*Rright\n  -(S+b)/(S+1)||e||^2-2(S+1)<e,w>\n  +[2(S+1)^2-(S+b)^2]||w||^2\n  +2(2S+b+1)Ny+2(S+1)Nt+2(S+b)/(S+1)NR.\n\nThe signed quadratic cannot be dropped. Its w coefficient is negative\nat every actual parent depth>=3 for these same-m parameters.\nThere is a REAL counterexample, not just an indefinite formal expression:\n\n Cchild_j=1+S_j, k_j=a*c^j, r_j=k_j/Cchild_j,\n D=[-r_j,1], x0=Cchild_j, actual word H_(j+1).\n\nEvery left query has gradient1, its endpoint is1; the central step gives\n-k_j; every right query has gradient-r_j, and terminal is-r_j. All guards\nhold including both boundary endpoints. In the gluing identity both child\nresidues, e, and all three normals vanish. The shifted-norm law fails iff\n k_j/Cchild_j>sqrt2-1.\nWriting v=a/(c-2),\n Cchild_j=(1-v)2^j+v*c^j,\nshows that this ratio increases with j.
